# Tier Index - Tier 2

This index lists every note currently classified as **Tier 2** under the Chief Archivist Protocol. It is a navigation aid, not a canon rewrite.

## Purpose

Use this page when you want to browse the archive by reader complexity rather than by subject. Tier 2 material covers civilizations, governments, militaries, social structures, locations, and continuity material that benefits from basic archive orientation.

## Q&A Support

Use [[VI - Q&A/VI - Q&A|VI - Q&A]] when a reader needs a plain-language bridge before opening notes in this tier.
